Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..20)
find y: int(0..5)
find z: int(0..6)

such that

!((x / (y / z)) = 10)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#int_rank_1: bool
  find x#int_rank_2: bool
  find x#int_rank_3: bool
  find x#int_rank_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#int_rank_4),or([!(x#int_rank_3),or([!(x#int_rank_2),or([!(x#int_rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

y
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
new variables:
  find y#int_rank_1: bool
  find y#int_rank_2: bool
  find y#int_rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(y#int_rank_3),and([!(y#int_rank_2),or([!(y#int_rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#int_rank_1: bool
  find z#int_rank_2: bool
  find z#int_rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#int_rank_3),or([!(z#int_rank_2),and([!(z#int_rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

x
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(5, 20)]), [x#int_rank_1,x#int_rank_2,x#int_rank_3,x#int_rank_4;int(1..)] [5, 20])

--

y
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#int_rank_1,y#int_rank_2,y#int_rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5])

--

z
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#int_rank_1,z#int_rank_2,z#int_rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6])

--

!(z#int_rank_3)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> !(z#int_rank_3)) using tseitin

--

!(y#int_rank_3)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__1
new variables:
  findAux __1: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__1 <-> !(y#int_rank_3)) using tseitin

--

!(z#int_rank_2)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__2

   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__3
new variables:
  findAux __3: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__3 <-> !(y#int_rank_2)) using tseitin

--

!(z#int_rank_1)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
__4
new variables:
  findAux __4: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__4 <-> !(z#int_rank_1)) using tseitin

--

or([__0,or([__2,and([__4;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> normalise_associative_commutative ([("Base", 8900)])
or([__0,__2,and([__4;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

and([__4;int(1..)])
   ~~> remove_unit_vector_and ([("Base", 8800)])
__4

and([__3;int(1..)])
   ~~> remove_unit_vector_and ([("Base", 8800)])
__3

or([__0,__2,__4;int(1..)])
   ~~> apply_tseytin_and_or ([("SAT", 8500)])

  define(__5 <-> or([__0,__2,__4;int(1..)])) using tseitin

   ~~> apply_tseytin_and_or ([("SAT", 8500)])
